========================================================
Original
========================================================
module test;
using system::*;

class ChainedObserveNode
{
	prop Child : ChainedObserveNode^ = null {}
	prop Value : int = 0 {}
	event Refreshed();
}

func TraceChanges(root : ChainedObserveNode^, subscription : Subscription^) : string
{
	var first = new ChainedObserveNode^();
	var second = new ChainedObserveNode^();
	root.Child = first;

	var trace = {""};
	attach(subscription.ValueChanged, func(value : object) : void
	{
		trace[0] = trace[0] & $"[$(cast int value)]";
	});
	subscription.Open();

	first.Value = 1;
	second.Value = 2;
	root.Child = second;
	first.Value = 3;
	first.Refreshed();
	second.Value = 4;
	second.Refreshed();
	root.Child = null;
	second.Value = 5;
	root.Child = first;
	first.Value = 6;

	subscription.Close();
	first.Value = 7;
	return trace[0];
}

func main() : string
{
	var root = new ChainedObserveNode^();
	var properties = TraceChanges(root, bind(root.Child.Value ?? -1));
	var observations = TraceChanges(root, bind(
		root.observe as parent(parent.Child on parent.ChildChanged)
			.observe as child(child is null ? -1 : child.Value on child.ValueChanged, child.Refreshed)
	));
	return properties & "/" & observations;
}

module test;
using system::*;

class ChainedObserveNode
{
    @cpp:Private
    var <prop>Child : ChainedObserveNode^ = null;

    func GetChild() : (ChainedObserveNode^)
    {
        return <prop>Child;
    }

    func SetChild(<value> : ChainedObserveNode^) : (void)
    {
        if ((<prop>Child != <value>))
        {
            (<prop>Child = <value>);
            ChildChanged();
        }
    }

    event ChildChanged();

    prop Child : ChainedObserveNode^{GetChild, SetChild : ChildChanged}

    @cpp:Private
    var <prop>Value : int = 0;

    func GetValue() : (int)
    {
        return <prop>Value;
    }

    func SetValue(<value> : int) : (void)
    {
        if ((<prop>Value != <value>))
        {
            (<prop>Value = <value>);
            ValueChanged();
        }
    }

    event ValueChanged();

    prop Value : int{GetValue, SetValue : ValueChanged}

    event Refreshed();

    new ()
    {
    }
}

func TraceChanges(root : ChainedObserveNode^, subscription : Subscription^) : (string)
{
    var first = new (ChainedObserveNode^)();
    var second = new (ChainedObserveNode^)();
    (root.Child = first);
    var trace = {"";};
    attach(subscription.ValueChanged, func (value : object) : (void)
    {
        (trace[0] = (trace[0] & (("[" & (cast (int) value)) & "]")));
    });
    subscription.Open();
    (first.Value = 1);
    (second.Value = 2);
    (root.Child = second);
    (first.Value = 3);
    first.Refreshed();
    (second.Value = 4);
    second.Refreshed();
    (root.Child = null);
    (second.Value = 5);
    (root.Child = first);
    (first.Value = 6);
    subscription.Close();
    (first.Value = 7);
    return trace[0];
}
